On 21 September 2024, it was reported that Prime Minister Rama was planning to create the [Sovereign State of the Bektashi Order](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sovereign_State_of_the_Bektashi_Order "Sovereign State of the Bektashi Order"), a sovereign [microstate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Microstate "Microstate") for the Order within Tirana.[\[133\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-nyt_article-135)

## Environment

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=13 "Edit section: Environment")]

### Geography

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=14 "Edit section: Geography")]

Main article: [Geography of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geography_of_Albania "Geography of Albania")

[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0f/Valbona_nga_Kukaj.jpg/300px-Valbona_nga_Kukaj.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Valbona_nga_Kukaj.jpg)

The [Albanian Alps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Alps "Albanian Alps") are an extension and simultaneously the highest section of the [Dinaric Alps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dinaric_Alps "Dinaric Alps").

Albania lies along the [Mediterranean Sea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_Sea "Mediterranean Sea") on the [Balkan Peninsula](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balkan_Peninsula "Balkan Peninsula") in [South](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Southern_Europe "Southern Europe") and [Southeast Europe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Southeast_Europe "Southeast Europe"), and has an area of 28,748 km2 (11,100 sq mi).[\[134\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-Albania_pdf-136) It is bordered by the [Adriatic Sea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adriatic_Sea "Adriatic Sea") to the west, [Montenegro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Montenegro "Montenegro") to the northwest, [Kosovo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kosovo "Kosovo") to the northeast, [North Macedonia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/North_Macedonia "North Macedonia") to the east, [Greece](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greece "Greece") to the south, and the [Ionian Sea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ionian_Sea "Ionian Sea") to the southwest. It is between latitudes [42°](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/42nd_parallel_north "42nd parallel north") and [39° N](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/39th_parallel_north "39th parallel north") and longitudes [21°](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/21st_meridian_east "21st meridian east") and [19° E](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/19th_meridian_east "19th meridian east"). Geographic coordinates include [Vërmosh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/V%C3%ABrmosh "Vërmosh") at 42° 35' 34" northern latitude as the northernmost point, [Konispol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Konispol "Konispol") at 39° 40' 0" northern latitude as the southernmost, [Sazan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sazan_Island "Sazan Island") at 19° 16' 50" eastern longitude as the westernmost, and [Vërnik](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/V%C3%ABrnik "Vërnik") at 21° 1' 26" eastern longitude as the easternmost.[\[135\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-137) [Mount Korab](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mount_Korab "Mount Korab"), rising at 2,764 m (9,068.24 ft) [above the Adriatic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metres_above_the_Adriatic "Metres above the Adriatic"), is the highest point, while the Mediterranean Sea, at 0 m (0.00 ft), is the lowest. The country extends 148 km (92 mi) from east to west and around 340 km (211 mi) from north to south.

Albania has a diverse and varied landscape with mountains and hills that traverse its territory in various directions. The country is home to extensive mountain ranges, including the [Albanian Alps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Alps "Albanian Alps") in the north, the [Korab Mountains](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Korab "Korab") in the east, the [Pindus Mountains](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pindus_Mountains "Pindus Mountains") in the southeast, the [Ceraunian Mountains](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ceraunian_Mountains "Ceraunian Mountains") in the southwest, and the [Skanderbeg Mountains](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Skanderbeg_Mountains "Skanderbeg Mountains") in the centre. In the northwest is the [Lake of Shkodër](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lake_of_Shkod%C3%ABr "Lake of Shkodër"), Southern Europe's largest lake.[\[136\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-138) Towards the [southeast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Ohrid_Lake_Coast "Albanian Ohrid Lake Coast") emerges the [Lake of Ohrid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lake_of_Ohrid "Lake of Ohrid"), one of the world's oldest continuously existing lakes.[\[137\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-139) Farther south, the expanse includes the [Large](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prespa_Lake "Prespa Lake") and [Small Lake of Prespa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Small_Prespa_Lake "Small Prespa Lake"), some of the Balkans' highest lakes. Rivers rise mostly in the east and discharge into the Adriatic and Ionian Seas. The country's longest river, measured from mouth to source, is the [Drin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Drin_River "Drin River"), which starts at the confluence of its two headwaters, the [Black](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_Drin "Black Drin") and [White Drin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_Drin "White Drin"). Of particular concern is the [Vjosë](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vjos%C3%AB "Vjosë"), one of Europe's last intact large river systems.

In Albania [forest cover](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forest_cover "Forest cover") is around 29.% of the total land area, equivalent to 788,900 hectares (ha) of forest in 2020, up from 788,800 hectares (ha) in 1990. Of the naturally regenerating forest 11% was reported to be [primary forest](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Primary_forest "Primary forest") (consisting of native tree species with no clearly visible indications of human activity) and around 0% of the forest area was found within protected areas. For the year 2015, 97% of the forest area was reported to be under [public ownership](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/State_ownership "State ownership"), 3% [private ownership](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Private_property "Private property") and 0% with ownership listed as other or unknown.[\[138\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-140)[\[139\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-141)

### Climate

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=15 "Edit section: Climate")]

Main article: [Climate of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Climate_of_Albania "Climate of Albania")

[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/11/Gjipe_beach%2C_Albania.JPG/220px-Gjipe_beach%2C_Albania.JPG)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Gjipe_beach,_Albania.JPG)

[Gjipe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gjipe "Gjipe") is located on the confluence of the [Adriatic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adriatic_Sea "Adriatic Sea") and [Ionian Sea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ionian_Sea "Ionian Sea"), on the [Albanian Riviera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Riviera "Albanian Riviera")

The [climate of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Climate_of_Albania "Climate of Albania") exhibits a distinguished level of variability and diversity due to the differences in latitude, longitude and altitude.[\[140\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate-142)[\[141\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate2-143) Albania experiences a [Mediterranean](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_climate "Mediterranean climate") and [Continental climate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Continental_climate "Continental climate"), characterised by the presence of four distinct seasons.[\[142\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate1-144) According to the [Köppen classification](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/K%C3%B6ppen_climate_classification "Köppen climate classification"), Albania encompasses five primary climatic types, spanning from Mediterranean and [subtropical](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Humid_subtropical_climate "Humid subtropical climate") in the western half to [oceanic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oceanic_climate "Oceanic climate"), [continental](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Humid_continental_climate "Humid continental climate") and [subarctic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Subarctic_climate "Subarctic climate") in the eastern half of the country.[\[143\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-145) The coastal regions along the [Adriatic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Adriatic_Sea_Coast "Albanian Adriatic Sea Coast") and [Ionian Seas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Ionian_Sea_Coast "Albanian Ionian Sea Coast") in Albania are acknowledged as the warmest areas, while the northern and eastern regions encompassing the [Albanian Alps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Alps "Albanian Alps") and the [Korab Mountains](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Korab_Mountains "Korab Mountains") are recognised as the coldest areas in the country.[\[144\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate3-146) Throughout the year, the average monthly temperatures fluctuate, ranging from −1 [°C](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Celsius "Celsius") (30 [°F](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fahrenheit "Fahrenheit")) during the winter months to 21.8 °C (71.2 °F) in the summer months. Notably, the highest recorded temperature of 43.9 °C (111.0 °F) was observed in [Kuçovë](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ku%C3%A7ov%C3%AB "Kuçovë") on 18 July 1973, while the lowest temperature of −29 °C (−20 °F) was recorded in Shtyllë, [Librazhd](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Librazhd "Librazhd") on 9 January 2017.[\[145\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-147)[\[146\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-148)

Albania receives most of the [precipitation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Precipitation_\(meteorology\) "Precipitation (meteorology)") in winter months and less in summer months.[\[141\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate2-143) The average precipitation is about 1,485 millimetres (58.5 inches).[\[144\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate3-146) The mean annual precipitation ranges between 600 and 3,000 millimetres (24 and 118 inches) depending on geographical location.[\[142\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate1-144) The [northwestern](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northern_Mountain_Range_\(Albania\) "Northern Mountain Range (Albania)") and [southeastern highlands](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Southern_Mountain_Range_\(Albania\) "Southern Mountain Range (Albania)") receive the intenser amount of precipitation, whilst the [northeastern](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northern_Mountain_Range_\(Albania\) "Northern Mountain Range (Albania)") and [southwestern highlands](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Southern_Mountain_Range_\(Albania\) "Southern Mountain Range (Albania)") as well as the [Western Lowlands](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Western_Lowlands "Western Lowlands") the more limited amount.[\[144\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate3-146) The [Albanian Alps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Alps "Albanian Alps") in the far north of the country are considered to be among the most humid regions of Europe, receiving at least 3,100 mm (122.0 in) of rain annually.[\[144\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-climate3-146) Four [glaciers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glacier "Glacier") within these mountains were discovered at a relatively low altitude of 2,000 metres (6,600 ft), which is extremely rare for such a southerly latitude.[\[147\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-149)

### Biodiversity

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=16 "Edit section: Biodiversity")]

Main article: [Biodiversity of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Biodiversity_of_Albania "Biodiversity of Albania")

See also: [Ecoregions in Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_ecoregions_in_Albania "List of ecoregions in Albania")

[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/5b/Golden_eagle_%2813434882845%29.jpg/220px-Golden_eagle_%2813434882845%29.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Golden_eagle_\(13434882845\).jpg)

The [golden eagle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golden_eagle "Golden eagle") is the national symbol and animal of Albania

A [biodiversity hotspot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Biodiversity_hotspot "Biodiversity hotspot"), Albania possesses an exceptionally rich and contrasting [biodiversity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Biodiversity "Biodiversity") on account of its geographical location at the centre of the [Mediterranean Sea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_Sea "Mediterranean Sea") and the great diversity in its [climatic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Climatic "Climatic"), geological and [hydrological](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hydrological "Hydrological") conditions.[\[148\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-150)[\[149\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-151) Because of remoteness, the mountains and hills of Albania are endowed with forests, trees and grasses that are essential to the lives for a wide variety of animals, among others for two of the most [endangered species](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Endangered_species "Endangered species") of the country, the [lynx](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balkan_lynx "Balkan lynx") and [brown bear](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brown_bear "Brown bear"), as well as the [wildcat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wildcat "Wildcat"), [grey wolf](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Grey_wolf "Grey wolf"), [red fox](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_fox "Red fox"), [golden jackal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golden_jackal "Golden jackal"), [Egyptian vulture](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Egyptian_vulture "Egyptian vulture") and [golden eagle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golden_eagle "Golden eagle"), the latter constituting the national animal of the country.[\[150\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-152)[\[151\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-153)[\[152\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-154)[\[153\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-155)

The estuaries, wetlands and lakes are extraordinarily important for the [greater flamingo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greater_flamingo "Greater flamingo"), [pygmy cormorant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pygmy_cormorant "Pygmy cormorant") and the extremely rare and perhaps the most iconic bird of the country, the [dalmatian pelican](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dalmatian_pelican "Dalmatian pelican").[\[154\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-156) Of particular importance are the [Mediterranean monk seal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_monk_seal "Mediterranean monk seal"), [loggerhead sea turtle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Loggerhead_sea_turtle "Loggerhead sea turtle") and [green sea turtle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Green_sea_turtle "Green sea turtle") that use to nest on the country's coastal waters and shores.

In terms of [phytogeography](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Phytogeography "Phytogeography"), Albania is part of the [Boreal Kingdom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boreal_Kingdom "Boreal Kingdom") and stretches specifically within the [Illyrian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Illyria "Illyria") province of the [Circumboreal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Circumboreal_Region "Circumboreal Region") and [Mediterranean Region](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_Basin "Mediterranean Basin"). Its territory can be subdivided into four terrestrial [ecoregions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ecoregion "Ecoregion") of the [Palearctic realm](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Palearctic_realm "Palearctic realm") namely within the [Illyrian deciduous forests](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Illyrian_deciduous_forests "Illyrian deciduous forests"), [Balkan mixed forests](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balkan_mixed_forests "Balkan mixed forests"), [Pindus Mountains mixed forests](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pindus_Mountains_mixed_forests "Pindus Mountains mixed forests") and [Dinaric Mountains mixed forests](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dinaric_Mountains_mixed_forests "Dinaric Mountains mixed forests").[\[155\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-157)[\[156\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-158)

Approximately 3,500 different species of plants can be found in Albania which refers principally to a [Mediterranean](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean "Mediterranean") and [Eurasian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eurasia "Eurasia") character. The country maintains a vibrant tradition of herbal and medicinal practices. At the minimum 300 plants growing locally are used in the preparation of herbs and medicines.[\[157\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-159) The trees within the forests are primarily [fir](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fir "Fir"), [oak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oak "Oak"), [beech](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beech "Beech") and [pine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pine "Pine").

## Conservation

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=17 "Edit section: Conservation")]

[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/0e/Ksamill-1.jpg/220px-Ksamill-1.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Ksamill-1.jpg)

The [islets of Ksamil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islets_of_Ksamil "Islets of Ksamil") lie in the [Butrint National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Butrint_National_Park "Butrint National Park")

Albania has been an active participant in numerous international agreements and conventions aimed at strengthing its commitment to the preservation and sustainable management of biological diversity. Since 1994, the country is a party to the [Convention on Biological Diversity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Convention_on_Biological_Diversity "Convention on Biological Diversity") (CBD) and its associated [Cartagena](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cartagena_Protocol_on_Biosafety "Cartagena Protocol on Biosafety") and [Nagoya Protocols](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nagoya_Protocol "Nagoya Protocol").[\[158\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-CBD-160) To uphold these commitments, it has developed and implemented a comprehensive [National Biodiversity Strategy and Action Plan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Biodiversity_action_plan "Biodiversity action plan") (NBSAP).[\[158\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-CBD-160) Furthermore, Albania has established a partnership with the [International Union for Conservation of Nature](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Union_for_Conservation_of_Nature "International Union for Conservation of Nature") (IUCN), advancing its conservation efforts on both national and international scales. Guided by the IUCN, the country has made substantial progress in the foundation of [protected areas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Protected_area "Protected area") within its boundaries, encompassing 12 [national parks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_park "National park") among others [Butrint](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Butrint_National_Park "Butrint National Park"), [Karaburun-Sazan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karaburun-Sazan_Marine_Park "Karaburun-Sazan Marine Park"), [Llogara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Llogara_National_Park "Llogara National Park"), [Prespa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prespa_National_Park_\(Albania\) "Prespa National Park (Albania)") and [Vjosa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vjosa_Wild_River_National_Park "Vjosa Wild River National Park").[\[159\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-New_NP-161)

As a signatory to the [Ramsar Convention](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ramsar_Convention "Ramsar Convention"), Albania has granted special recognition upon four wetlands, designating them as Wetlands of International Importance, including [Buna](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Buna_\(Adriatic_Sea\) "Buna (Adriatic Sea)")-[Shkodër](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lake_Skadar "Lake Skadar"), [Butrint](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Butrint_Lagoon "Butrint Lagoon"), [Karavasta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karavasta_Lagoon "Karavasta Lagoon") and [Prespa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lake_Prespa "Lake Prespa").[\[160\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-162) The country's dedication to protection extends further into the sphere of [UNESCO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UNESCO "UNESCO")'s [World Network of Biosphere Reserves](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Network_of_Biosphere_Reserves "World Network of Biosphere Reserves"), operating within the framework of the [Man and the Biosphere Programme](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Man_and_the_Biosphere_Programme "Man and the Biosphere Programme"), evidenced by its engagement in the [Ohrid-Prespa Transboundary Biosphere Reserve](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ohrid-Prespa_Transboundary_Biosphere_Reserve "Ohrid-Prespa Transboundary Biosphere Reserve").[\[161\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-163)[\[162\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-164) Furthermore, Albania is host to two natural [World Heritage Sites](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Heritage_Site "World Heritage Site"), which encompass the Ohrid region and both the [Gashi River](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gashi_River "Gashi River") and [Rrajca](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rrajce "Rrajce") as part of [Ancient and Primeval Beech Forests of the Carpathians and Other Regions of Europe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ancient_and_Primeval_Beech_Forests_of_the_Carpathians_and_Other_Regions_of_Europe "Ancient and Primeval Beech Forests of the Carpathians and Other Regions of Europe").[\[163\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-Ancient_Primeval_Beech_Forests-165)

### Protected areas

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=18 "Edit section: Protected areas")]

Main article: [Protected areas of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Protected_areas_of_Albania "Protected areas of Albania")

[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/42/A_fishermen_house_in_Karavasta_Lagoon_%28Divjak%C3%AB-Karavasta_National_Park%29.jpg/220px-A_fishermen_house_in_Karavasta_Lagoon_%28Divjak%C3%AB-Karavasta_National_Park%29.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:A_fishermen_house_in_Karavasta_Lagoon_\(Divjak%C3%AB-Karavasta_National_Park\).jpg)

The [lagoon of Karavasta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lagoon_of_Karavasta "Lagoon of Karavasta") within the [Divjakë-Karavasta National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Divjak%C3%AB-Karavasta_National_Park "Divjakë-Karavasta National Park")

The [protected areas of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Protected_areas_of_Albania "Protected areas of Albania") are areas designated and managed by the [Albanian government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_government "Albanian government"). There are 12 [national parks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_park "National park"), 4 [ramsar sites](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ramsar_site "Ramsar site"), 1 [biosphere reserve](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Biosphere_reserve "Biosphere reserve") and 786 other types of conservation reserves in Albania.[\[159\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-New_NP-161)[\[164\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-166) Located in the north, the Albanian Alps National Park, comprising the former [Theth National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Theth_National_Park "Theth National Park") and [Valbonë Valley National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Valbon%C3%AB_Valley_National_Park "Valbonë Valley National Park"), is surrounded amidst the towering peaks of the [Albanian Alps](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Accursed_Mountains "Accursed Mountains"). In the east, portions of the rugged [Korab](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Korab "Korab"), [Nemërçka](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nem%C3%ABr%C3%A7ka "Nemërçka") and [Shebenik](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shebenik "Shebenik") Mountains are conserved within the boundaries of [Fir of Hotovë-Dangëlli National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fir_of_Hotov%C3%AB-Dang%C3%ABlli_National_Park "Fir of Hotovë-Dangëlli National Park"), [Shebenik National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shebenik_National_Park "Shebenik National Park") and [Prespa National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prespa_National_Park_\(Albania\) "Prespa National Park (Albania)"), with the latter encompassing Albania's share of the [Great](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prespa_Lake "Prespa Lake") and [Small Lakes of Prespa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Small_Prespa_Lake "Small Prespa Lake").

To the south, the [Ceraunian Mountains](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ceraunian_Mountains "Ceraunian Mountains") define the [Albanian Ionian Sea Coast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Ionian_Sea_Coast "Albanian Ionian Sea Coast"), shaping the landscape of [Llogara National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Llogara_National_Park "Llogara National Park"), which extends into the [Karaburun Peninsula](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karaburun_Peninsula,_Albania "Karaburun Peninsula, Albania"), forming the [Karaburun-Sazan Marine Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karaburun-Sazan_Marine_Park "Karaburun-Sazan Marine Park"). Further southward lies [Butrint National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Butrint_National_Park "Butrint National Park"), occupying a peninsula surrounded by the Lake of Butrint and the [Channel of Vivari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Channel_of_Vivari "Channel of Vivari"). In the west, stretching along the [Albanian Adriatic Sea Coast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Adriatic_Sea_Coast "Albanian Adriatic Sea Coast"), the [Divjakë-Karavasta National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Divjak%C3%AB-Karavasta_National_Park "Divjakë-Karavasta National Park") boasts the extensive [Lagoon of Karavasta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lagoon_of_Karavasta "Lagoon of Karavasta"), one of the largest lagoon systems in the Mediterranean Sea. Notably, Europe's first wild river national park, [Vjosa National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vjosa_Wild_River_National_Park "Vjosa Wild River National Park"), safeguards the [Vjosa River](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vjosa "Vjosa") and its primary tributaries, which originates in the [Pindus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pindus "Pindus") Mountains and flows to the Adriatic Sea. [Dajti Mountain National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dajti_Mountain_National_Park "Dajti Mountain National Park"), [Lurë-Dejë Mountain National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lur%C3%AB-Dej%C3%AB_Mountain_National_Park "Lurë-Dejë Mountain National Park") and [Tomorr Mountain National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tomorr_Mountain_National_Park "Tomorr Mountain National Park") protect the mountainous terrain of the centre of Albania, including the [Tomorr](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tomorr "Tomorr") and [Skanderbeg Mountains](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Skanderbeg_Mountains "Skanderbeg Mountains").

### Environmental issues

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=19 "Edit section: Environmental issues")]

Main article: [Environmental issues in Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Environmental_issues_in_Albania "Environmental issues in Albania")

Environmental issues in Albania notably encompass [air](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Air_pollution "Air pollution") and [water pollution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Water_pollution "Water pollution"), [climate change](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Climate_change_in_Albania "Climate change in Albania") impacts, [waste management](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Waste_management "Waste management") shortcomings, [biodiversity loss](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conservation_biology "Conservation biology") and imperative for [nature conservation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nature_conservation "Nature conservation").[\[165\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-167)[\[166\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-168)

Climate change is predicted to exert significant impacts on the quality of life in Albania.[\[167\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-169) Albania is one of the European countries most at risk and vulnerable to [natural disasters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Natural_disaster "Natural disaster").[\[168\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-170) Natural disasters, such as [floods](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flood "Flood"), [forest fires](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wildfire "Wildfire"), and [landslides](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Landslide "Landslide"), are increasing in Albania due to climate change, causing significant damage.[\[169\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-:12-171)[\[170\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-172) Rising sea levels are anticipated to negatively impact coastal communities and the tourism industry.[\[171\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-:13-173)

In 2023 Albania emitted 7.67 million tonnes of [greenhouse gases](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Greenhouse_gas "Greenhouse gas"),[\[172\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-174) equivalent to 2.73 tonnes per person,[\[173\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-175) making it a relatively low emitting country. Albania has pledged a 20.9% reduction in GHG emissions by 2030, and [net zero](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Net-zero_emissions "Net-zero emissions") by 2050.[\[174\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-176)

The country has a moderate and improving performance in the [Environmental Performance Index](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Environmental_Performance_Index "Environmental Performance Index") with an overall ranking of 62 out of 180 countries in 2022.[\[175\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-177) However, Albania's ranking has decreased since its highest placement at position 15 in the Environmental Performance Index of 2012.[\[176\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-178)

[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/02/Prespa_and_Prespa_National_Park_Albania_2017.jpg/1016px-Prespa_and_Prespa_National_Park_Albania_2017.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Prespa_and_Prespa_National_Park_Albania_2017.jpg "The Prespa National Park in southeastern Albania is part of the European Green Belt and Ohrid-Prespa Biosphere Reserve.")

[](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Prespa_and_Prespa_National_Park_Albania_2017.jpg "File:Prespa and Prespa National Park Albania 2017.jpg")

The [Prespa National Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prespa_National_Park_\(Albania\) "Prespa National Park (Albania)") in southeastern Albania is part of the [European Green Belt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Green_Belt "European Green Belt") and [Ohrid-Prespa Biosphere Reserve](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ohrid-Prespa_Transboundary_Biosphere_Reserve "Ohrid-Prespa Transboundary Biosphere Reserve").

## Politics

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=20 "Edit section: Politics")]

Main article: [Politics of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Politics_of_Albania "Politics of Albania")

See also: [Government of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_of_Albania "Government of Albania")

|                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/57/Bajram_Begaj_%28infobox_crop%29.jpg/108px-Bajram_Begaj_%28infobox_crop%29.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Bajram_Begaj_\(infobox_crop\).jpg) | [![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/de/Edi_Rama_%282024-02-29%29.jpg/120px-Edi_Rama_%282024-02-29%29.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Edi_Rama_\(2024-02-29\).jpg) |
| [Bajram Begaj](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bajram_Begaj "Bajram Begaj") [President](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President_of_Albania "President of Albania")                                                     | [Edi Rama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edi_Rama "Edi Rama") [Prime Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Albania "Prime Minister of Albania")                                |

Since declaring independence in 1912, Albania has experienced a significant political transformation, traversing through distinct periods that included a monarchical rule, a communist regime and the eventual establishment of a democratic order.[\[177\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-179) In 1998, Albania transitioned into a [sovereign](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sovereign_state "Sovereign state") [parliamentary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Parliamentary_republic "Parliamentary republic") [constitutional republic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Constitutional_republic "Constitutional republic"), marking a fundamental milestone in its political evolution.[\[178\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-Kushtetuta-180) Its governance structure operates under a [constitution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Constitution_of_Albania "Constitution of Albania") that serves as the principal document of the country.[\[179\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-181) The constitution is grounded in the principle of the [separation of powers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Separation_of_powers "Separation of powers"), with three arms of government that encompass the legislative embodied in the [Parliament](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Parliament_of_Albania "Parliament of Albania"), the executive led by the [President](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President_of_Albania "President of Albania") as the ceremonial [head of state](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Head_of_state "Head of state") and the [Prime Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Albania "Prime Minister of Albania") as the functional [head of government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Head_of_government "Head of government"), and the judiciary with a hierarchy of courts, including the [constitutional](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Constitutional_Court_of_Albania "Constitutional Court of Albania") and [supreme courts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Supreme_Court_of_Albania "Supreme Court of Albania") as well as multiple [appeal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Appeals_courts_of_Albania "Appeals courts of Albania") and [administrative courts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Administrative_Courts_in_Albania "Administrative Courts in Albania").[\[178\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-Kushtetuta-180)

Albania's legal system is structured to protect its people's political rights, regardless of their ethnic, linguistic, racial, or religious affiliations.[\[178\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-Kushtetuta-180)[\[180\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-FH2023-182) Despite these principles, there are significant human rights concerns in Albania that demand attention.[\[181\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-USDS_Politics-183) These concerns include issues related to the independence of the judiciary, the absence of a free media sector and the enduring problem of corruption within various governmental bodies, law enforcement agencies and other institutions.[\[181\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-USDS_Politics-183) As Albania pursues its path towards EU membership, active efforts are being made to achieve substantial improvements in these areas to align with EU criteria and standards.[\[180\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-FH2023-182)

### Foreign relations

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=21 "Edit section: Foreign relations")]

Main article: [Foreign relations of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foreign_relations_of_Albania "Foreign relations of Albania")

[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/36/Artigianato_Arberesh.jpg/220px-Artigianato_Arberesh.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Artigianato_Arberesh.jpg)

Assisted by the governments of [Kosovo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kosovo "Kosovo") and Albania, an official application for the inclusion of the [Arbëreshë people](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arb%C3%ABresh%C3%AB_people "Arbëreshë people") in the list of [UNESCO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UNESCO "UNESCO")'s [Intangible Cultural Heritage](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UNESCO_Intangible_Cultural_Heritage_Lists "UNESCO Intangible Cultural Heritage Lists") is being prepared.[\[182\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-184)

Emerging from decades of isolation during the communism, Albania has adopted a [foreign policy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foreign_relations_of_Albania "Foreign relations of Albania") orientation centred on active cooperation and engagement in international affairs. At the core of Albania's foreign policies lie a set of objectives, which encompass the commitment to protect its sovereignty and territorial integrity, the cultivation of diplomatic ties with other countries, advocating for [international recognition of Kosovo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_recognition_of_Kosovo "International recognition of Kosovo"), addressing the concerns related to the [expulsion of Cham Albanians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Expulsion_of_Cham_Albanians "Expulsion of Cham Albanians"), pursuing Euro-Atlantic integration and protecting the rights of the Albanians in [Kosovo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanians_in_Kosovo "Albanians in Kosovo"), [Greece](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanians_in_greece "Albanians in greece"), [Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arb%C3%ABresh%C3%AB_people "Arbëreshë people"), [Montenegro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanians_in_Montenegro "Albanians in Montenegro"), [North Macedonia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanians_in_Macedonia "Albanians in Macedonia"), [Serbia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanians_in_south_Serbia "Albanians in south Serbia") and the [diaspora](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_diaspora "Albanian diaspora").[\[183\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-185)

The external affairs of Albania underscore the country's dedication to regional stability and integration into major international institutions.[\[184\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-186) Albania became a member of the [United Nations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations "United Nations") (UN) in 1955, shortly after emerging from a period of isolation during the communist era.[\[185\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-187) The country reached a major achievement in its foreign policy by securing NATO membership in 2009.[\[186\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-188)[\[187\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-189) Since obtaining candidate status in 2014, the country has also embarked on a comprehensive reform agenda to align itself with European Union (EU) accession standards, with the objective of becoming an EU member state.[\[118\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-EU_CS-120)

Albania and Kosovo maintain a fraternal relationship strengthened by their substantial cultural, ethnical and historical ties.[\[188\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-AlbKosRelation-190) Both countries foster enduring diplomatic ties, with Albania actively supporting Kosovo's development and international integration efforts.[\[188\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-AlbKosRelation-190) Its fundamental contribution to [Kosovo's path to independence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2008_Kosovo_declaration_of_independence "2008 Kosovo declaration of independence") is underscored by its early [recognition of Kosovo's sovereignty](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_recognition_of_Kosovo "International recognition of Kosovo") in 2008.[\[189\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-191) Furthermore, both governments hold annual joint meetings, displayed by the inaugural meeting in 2014, which serves as an official platform to enhance bilateral cooperation and reinforce their joint commitment to policies that promote the stability and prosperity of the broader Albanian region.[\[188\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-AlbKosRelation-190)

### Military

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=22 "Edit section: Military")]

Main article: [Armed Forces of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Armed_Forces_of_Albania "Armed Forces of Albania")

[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1f/Albanian_special_operations_forces%2C_provide_security_as_Afghan_Border_Police_%28ABP%29_break_ground_on_a_new_checkpoint_in_the_district_of_Spin_Boldak%2C_Kandahar_province%2C_Afghanistan%2C_March_25%2C_2013_130325-A-MX357-127.jpg/220px-thumbnail.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Albanian_special_operations_forces,_provide_security_as_Afghan_Border_Police_\(ABP\)_break_ground_on_a_new_checkpoint_in_the_district_of_Spin_Boldak,_Kandahar_province,_Afghanistan,_March_25,_2013_130325-A-MX357-127.jpg)

[Albanian soldiers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Land_Force "Albanian Land Force") in the [Province of Kandahar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kandahar_Province "Kandahar Province"), Afghanistan

The [Albanian Armed Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Armed_Forces "Albanian Armed Forces") consist of [Land](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Land_Force "Albanian Land Force"), [Air](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Air_Force "Albanian Air Force") and [Naval Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Naval_Force "Albanian Naval Force") and constitute the military and paramilitary forces of the country. They are led by a [commander-in-chief](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Commander-in-chief "Commander-in-chief") under the supervision of the [Ministry of Defence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Defence_\(Albania\) "Ministry of Defence (Albania)") and by the [President](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President_of_Albania "President of Albania") as the supreme commander during wartime. However, in times of peace its powers are executed through the [Prime Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Albania "Prime Minister of Albania") and the [Defence Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Defence_\(Albania\) "Ministry of Defence (Albania)").[\[190\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-constitution-192)

The chief purpose of the armed forces of Albania is the defence of the independence, the [sovereignty](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sovereignty "Sovereignty") and the [territorial integrity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Territorial_integrity "Territorial integrity") of the country, as well as the participation in humanitarian, combat, non-combat and peace support operations.[\[190\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-constitution-192) [Military service](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Military_service "Military service") is voluntary since 2010 with the age of 19 being the legal minimum age for the duty.[\[191\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-193)[\[192\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-194)

Albania has committed to increase the participations in multinational operations.[\[193\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-195) Since the fall of communism, the country has participated in six international missions but only one United Nations mission in [Georgia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UNOMIG "UNOMIG"), where it sent three military observers. Since February 2008, Albania has participated officially in NATO's [Operation Active Endeavor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operation_Active_Endeavor "Operation Active Endeavor") in the [Mediterranean Sea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediterranean_Sea "Mediterranean Sea").[\[194\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-196) It was invited to join NATO on 3 April 2008, and it became a full member on 2 April 2009.[\[195\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-197)

Albania reduced the number of active troops from 65,000 in 1988 to 14,500 in 2009.[\[196\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-198)[\[197\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-199) The military now consists mainly of a small fleet of aircraft and sea vessels. Increasing the military budget was one of the most important conditions for [NATO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NATO "NATO") integration. As of 1996 military spending was an estimated 1.5% of the country's GDP, only to peak in 2009 at 2% and fall again to 1.5%.[\[198\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-200)

## Administrative divisions

\[[edit](https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Albania\&action=edit\&section=23 "Edit section: Administrative divisions")]

Main article: [Administrative divisions of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Administrative_divisions_of_Albania "Administrative divisions of Albania")

See also: [Counties of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Counties_of_Albania "Counties of Albania"), [Regions of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Regions_of_Albania "Regions of Albania"), [Municipalities of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Municipalities_of_Albania "Municipalities of Albania"), [Communes of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communes_of_Albania "Communes of Albania"), and [Villages of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Villages_of_Albania "Villages of Albania")

[![Map of Albania](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/4e/Albania_%28location_map%29.svg/450px-Albania_%28location_map%29.svg.png)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Albania_\(location_map\).svg)

[Berat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Berat_County "Berat County")

[Dibër](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dib%C3%ABr_County "Dibër County")

[Durrës](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Durr%C3%ABs_County "Durrës County")

[Elbasan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elbasan_County "Elbasan County")

[Fier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fier_County "Fier County")

[Gjirokastër](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gjirokast%C3%ABr_County "Gjirokastër County")

[Lezhë](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lezh%C3%AB_County "Lezhë County")

[Korçë](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kor%C3%A7%C3%AB_County "Korçë County")

[Kukës](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kuk%C3%ABs_County "Kukës County")

[Shkodër](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shkod%C3%ABr_County "Shkodër County")

[Tirana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tirana_County "Tirana County")

[Vlorë](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vlor%C3%AB_County "Vlorë County")

[Adriatic Sea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adriatic_Sea "Adriatic Sea")

[Ionian Sea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ionian_Sea "Ionian Sea")

The [counties of Albania](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Counties_of_Albania "Counties of Albania") are the first-level administrative units in Albania.

Albania is defined within a territorial area of 28,748 km2 (11,100 sq mi) in the [Balkan Peninsula](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balkan_Peninsula "Balkan Peninsula"). It is informally divided into three regions, the [Northern](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northern_Albania "Northern Albania"), [Central](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_Albania "Central Albania") and [Southern Regions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Southern_Albania "Southern Albania"). Since [its Declaration of Independence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Albanian_Declaration_of_Independence "Albanian Declaration of Independence") in 1912, Albania has reformed [its internal organisation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Administrative_divisions_of_Albania#History "Administrative divisions of Albania") 21 times. Presently, the primary [administrative units](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Administrative_divisions_of_Albania "Administrative divisions of Albania") are the twelve constituent [counties](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Counties_of_Albania "Counties of Albania") (*[qarqe](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/qarqe#Albanian "wikt:qarqe")/[qarqet](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/qarqet#Albanian "wikt:qarqet")*), which hold equal status under the law.[\[199\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-201) Counties had previously been used in the 1950s and were recreated on 31 July 2000 to unify the 36 [districts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Districts_of_Albania "Districts of Albania") (*[rrathë](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/rrath%C3%AB#Albanian "wikt:rrathë")/[rrathët](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/rrath%C3%ABt#Albanian "wikt:rrathët")*) of that time.[\[200\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-202)[\[201\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-terref-203) The largest county in Albania by population is [Tirana County](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tirana_County "Tirana County") with over 800,000 people. The smallest county, by population, is [Gjirokastër County](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gjirokast%C3%ABr_County "Gjirokastër County") with over 70,000 people. The largest county, by area, is [Korçë County](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kor%C3%A7%C3%AB_County "Korçë County") encompassing 3,711 square kilometres (1,433 sq mi) of the southeast of Albania. The smallest county, by area, is [Durrës County](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Durr%C3%ABs_County "Durrës County") with an area of 766 square kilometres (296 sq mi) in the west of Albania.

The counties are made up of 61 second-level divisions known as [municipalities](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Municipalities_of_Albania "Municipalities of Albania") (*[bashki](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/bashki#Albanian "wikt:bashki")/[bashkia](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/bashkia#Albanian "wikt:bashkia")*).[\[202\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-204) The municipalities are the first level of local governance, responsible for local needs and [law enforcement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Law_enforcement_in_Albania "Law enforcement in Albania").[\[203\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-205)[\[204\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-206)[\[205\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-207) They unified and simplified the previous system of [urban](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Urban_municipalities_of_Albania "Urban municipalities of Albania") and [rural municipalities](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rural_municipalities_of_Albania "Rural municipalities of Albania") or [communes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Communes_of_Albania "Communes of Albania") (*[komuna](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/komuna#Albanian "wikt:komuna")/[komunat](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/komunat#Albanian "wikt:komunat")*) in 2015.[\[206\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-208)[\[207\]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/#cite_note-209) For smaller issues of [local government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Local_government "Local government"), the municipalities are organised into 373 [administrative units](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Administrative_units_of_Albania "Administrative units of Albania") (*njësia*/*njësitë administrative*). There are also 2980 villages (*[fshatra](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/fshatra#Albanian "wikt:fshatra")/[fshatrat](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/fshatrat#Albanian "wikt:fshatrat")*), neighborhoods or wards (*[lagje](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/lagje#Albanian "wikt:lagje")/[lagjet](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/lagjet#Albanian "wikt:lagjet")*), and localities (*[lokalitete](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/lokalitete#Albanian "wikt:lokalitete")/[lokalitetet](https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/lokalitetet#Albanian "wikt:lokalitetet")*) previously used as administrative units.
